<h3><a href="https://x.com/FiniYang/status/2102246440961724515">Playing a Kitten Survival Game with Official Jev and Local Laya Models</a></h3>
<p>The author built a kitten survival game where the official Jev and local Laya (mlx) models each choose every step to protect the kitten. Across three levels tested three times each, official Jev cleared 3/9 runs (all from the food-finding level), while local Laya cleared 0/9. The takeaway: Jev is more accurate but slower, Laya is extremely fast but inaccurate.</p>
<p><strong>中文摘要：</strong>作者制作了一个小猫生存游戏，让官方 Jev 和本地 Laya (mlx) 模型各自选择每一步行动来保护小猫。测试三种关卡各三次，官方 Jev 通关 3/9 局（均来自找饭关），本地 Laya 通关 0/9 局。结论是 Jev 更准但慢，Laya 极快但不准。</p>
